### [88] [The intrinsic luminosity-decay correlation in subsamples of GRB X-ray afterglows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19332)
*S. P. R. Shilling,S. R. Oates,J. L. Racusin,B. Cenko,R. Gupta,P. Nuessle,M. Smith,G. P. Lamb,C. Turnbull*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于427个Swift/XRT观测的X射线余辉样本，研究了伽马射线暴余辉中早期光度与平均衰减速率之间的本征相关性，发现二者显著相关，且该相关性在长暴（LGRBs）中显著、在短暴（SGRBs）中不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已在射电、光学/UV、X射线和GeV波段观测到GRB余辉中早期光度与平均衰减速率之间存在相关性，即更明亮的余辉往往具有更高的平均衰减速率。本文旨在利用更大规模、更新后的X射线余辉样本（427个），对此相关性进行更精确的定量检验，并探讨其潜在的物理成因。

Method: 作者汇编了427个具有红移测量的Swift/XRT X射线余辉样本（覆盖20年观测），对每个GRB测量静止坐标系下200秒处的光度L_X,200s及该时刻之后的平均衰减指数α_X,>200s。使用Spearman秩相关系数检验相关性显著性，并进行线性回归拟合斜率。将样本细分为395个长暴（LGRBs）与32个短暴（SGRBs）子样本分别检验；另选取102个光变曲线采样良好且结束时间较晚的LGRBs子样本分析相关性的弥散度与强度。

Result: 全样本中L_X,200s与α_X,>200s显著相关，Spearman秩相关系数为0.54±0.04（≥3σ显著性），线性回归斜率为0.18±0.02。LGRBs子样本中该相关性在≥3σ水平显著，而SGRBs中不显著，与以往研究一致。在102个光变曲线采样良好的LGRBs子样本中，相关性弥散显著降低，强度增强至R_sp=0.80±0.04，斜率与全样本一致。

Conclusion: 该相关性可能源于观察者视线与喷流轴之间的几何效应（视角效应），或由某种调节GRB中心引擎能量释放速率的机制所导致。本研究为约束该相关性的物理成因提供了重要的观测依据。

Abstract: The intrinsic luminosity-decay correlation in gamma-ray burst (GRB) afterglows, between the early-time luminosity and the average rate of decay past this time, has previously been observed in the radio, optical/UV, X-ray and GeV wavebands and quantitatively shows that more luminous afterglows tend to have higher average rates of decay. We have compiled an updated sample of 427 X-ray afterglows with measured redshifts, observed with Swift/XRT over 20 years. For each GRB, we measure the luminosity at 200 seconds in the rest frame, $L_{\mathrm{X,200s}}$, and the average rate of decay from this time, $α_{X,>200s}$. We find these parameters are correlated with a Spearman's rank coefficient ($R_\mathrm{sp}$) of $0.54\pm0.04$ at a significance of $\geq3σ$ and a linear regression slope of $0.18\pm0.02$. We separate our sample into subsamples, including 395 long GRBs (LGRBs) and 32 short GRBs (SGRBs) and find evidence of the $L_{\mathrm{X,200s}}$-$α_{X,>200s}$ correlation at a significance of $\geq3σ$ in LGRBs but not in SGRBs, consistent with previous studies. In a subsample of 102 LGRBs with well-sampled light curves and late end times, we find that scatter in the correlation is significantly reduced and the strength increases to $R_\mathrm{sp}=0.80\pm0.04$ whilst the slope remains consistent with the full sample. We discuss our results and, briefly, their potential implications on constraining the cause of the correlation. Possible causes include geometric effects due to the angle between the observer and the jet-axis, or some mechanism that regulates the rate at which energy is released by the GRB central engine.

</details>


### [89] [Modeling Relativistic Tidal Disruptions of MESA Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19402)
*Liam M. Wang,Giovanni Maria Tomaselli,Zihan Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在Newtonian TDE模型基础上引入相对论修正，包括Kerr测地线、相对论潮汐场和相对论回落时间，发现恒星会更早被瓦解、回落时间更长，但黑洞自旋对回落时间影响可忽略，且计算成本依然低廉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潮汐瓦解事件（TDEs）的数值模拟计算开销巨大，已有的Newtonian模型基于MESA恒星的恒星扰动理论提供了一种替代方案，但缺乏相对论效应。本文旨在为该模型添加相对论修正，使其更准确地计算回落率，同时保持计算成本低廉。

Method: 在已有的Newtonian TDE模型中添加三项相对论修正：（1）赤道面Kerr测地线用于描述恒星轨道；（2）相对论潮汐场用于计算恒星变形；（3）相对论回落时间。模型基于MESA恒星结构并利用恒星扰动理论，而非进行完整的流体动力学模拟。

Result: 相比Newtonian情况，相对论修正导致恒星在其轨道上更早被瓦解，因此积累的物理变形更少；同时因瓦解时距黑洞更远，回落时间更长。黑洞自旋对回落时间的影响可忽略，除非轨道的近心点异常接近黑洞。

Conclusion: 该模型比Newtonian模型能更精确地计算回落率，同时保持了较低的计算成本，代码已在GitHub上开源，为TDE后续观测（如光变曲线和暂现源探测）提供了更可靠的理论预测工具。

Abstract: Tidal disruption events (TDEs) occur when a star passes so close to a black hole that its self-gravity is overcome by the external tidal field. As the star passes, it initially deforms, then is ripped apart, and some of its material eventually falls back on bound orbits, forming an accretion disk around the black hole. A Newtonian model of TDEs, based on stellar perturbation theory of MESA stars, was recently introduced as an alternative to computationally intensive hydrodynamical simulations. In this work, we add relativistic corrections to the model, incorporating equatorial Kerr geodesics, relativistic tidal fields, and relativistic fallback times. Compared to the Newtonian case, we find that stars are disrupted earlier in their orbit, which gives them less time to accumulate physical deformations. Additionally, we find that the increased distance from the black hole at the time of disruption makes the fallback time longer. However, the black hole spin has a negligible impact on fallback time, except for orbits with exceptionally close pericenter. Our results allow for a more accurate calculation of fallback rates than the Newtonian model, while also remaining computationally cheap. The code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [90] [Outflows in steep density gradients: diversity of behavior and implications for tidal disruption events and luminous fast blue optical transients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19512)
*Benjamin Amend,Michael Camilo,Eric R. Coughlin,Anna Y. Q. Ho,Jonathan Zrake*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在能量持续注入下爆炸流出物的动力学，重点分析了正向激波和反向激波在不同风与周围介质密度比条件下的演化行为，并区分了两种极端情况（f<<1和f>>1）下冲击波的加速或匀速运动特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功率极大的爆炸可能经历持续的能源注入，由一个中心引擎向周围气体吹出风，形成正向激波和反向激波。已有工作尚未系统厘清在不同的环境密度剖面下，这两类激波结构的演化规律，尤其是它们是否会加速还是保持匀速扩张。这直接关系到对潮汐瓦解事件喷流和明亮快速蓝色光学瞬变等天体物理现象的理解。

Method: 作者采用解析标度关系与一维激波捕获流体动力学模拟相结合的方法，研究了两种极端密度比限制（f<<1和f>>1）下流出物在环境密度剖面ρ_amb ∝ r^{-n}（其中2≤n≤3）中的演化，并分析了激波结构是加速还是匀速运动。

Result: 对于n>2，初始欠密流出物产生加速的正向激波，半径演化为R_s ∝ t^{3/(5-n)}。由于ρ_w ∝ r^{-2}，f随半径增大，导致反向激波风区相对接触不连续面收缩，正向激波向匀速扩张过渡，该过渡发生在t_dec时f约为几的量级。相反，初始f_0>>1的流出物不经历延长的加速阶段，在整个绝热演化中近似保持匀速。

Conclusion: 该工作明确了两种极端密度比情况下流出物动力学的根本差异，并推导出加速到匀速的转变时间标度，为解释环境具有陡峭密度剖面（n>2）的潮汐瓦解事件喷流和明亮快速蓝色光学瞬变提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Powerful explosions may undergo sustained energy injection as a central engine launches a wind into the surrounding gas, generating a forward and a reverse shock separated by a contact discontinuity. During the adiabatic phase, the dynamics depend strongly on the wind-to-ambient density ratio $f \equiv ρ_{\rm w} / ρ_{\rm a}$. For $f << 1$, the reverse shock lies well inside the contact discontinuity, and the mechanical energy deposited by the wind is retained in a radially extended, approximately isobaric shocked-wind region whose pressure drives the swept-up ambient shell. For $f \gg 1$, the reverse shock remains close to the contact, and the expansion is governed by the ram-pressure interaction between the freely expanding wind and the swept-up ambient gas. We use analytic scalings and one-dimensional shock-capturing hydrodynamic simulations to determine how outflows in these two limits evolve in ambient density profiles $ρ_{\rm a} \propto r^{-n}$, where $2 \leq n \leq 3$, and whether their shock structures accelerate or coast at constant velocity. For $n > 2$, initially underdense outflows produce accelerating forward shocks whose radii evolve as $R_{\rm s} \propto t^{3/(5-n)}$. Because $ρ_{\rm w} \propto r^{-2}$, f increases with radius, causing the reverse-shocked wind region to contract relative to the contact position as the forward shock transitions toward constant-velocity expansion. This occurs when $f \sim$ a few at $t_{\rm dec} \propto f_0^{1/(2-n)}$, where $f_0$ is the initial wind-to-ambient density ratio. By contrast, outflows initialized with $f_0 \gg 1$ do not develop an extended accelerating phase and remain approximately coasting throughout their adiabatic evolution. We discuss applications to tidal disruption event outflows and luminous fast blue optical transients, whose environments are often inferred to have steep density profiles with $n > 2$.

</details>


### [91] [Discovery of Three Glitches in the previously quiet pulsar PSR J1637$-$4642](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19555)
*Zhaoyi Wang,Chuwen Zheng,Yuhong Zhuang,Hanyu Zhang,Peng Liu,Zhonghao Tu,Ang Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了年轻脉冲星PSR J1637−4642中三次转动突跳（glitch）的发现与分析。基于2009至2024年间Murriyang射电望远镜的计时观测，探测到一次强突跳（Δν/ν≈2.7×10⁻⁶）和两次较小突跳，并利用涡旋蠕变框架进行贝叶斯建模，推断出超流体转动惯量占比约0.0187，与内壳层超流体一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该脉冲星自Parkes多波束巡天发现以来从未表现出突跳活动，属于"安静"脉冲星。研究其罕见的突跳行为有助于检验标准超流体突跳范式，并深入理解脉冲星内部超流体结构与转动演化机制。

Method: 利用Parkes天文台Murriyang射电望远镜在MJD 54881−60589期间的计时观测数据，通过计时分析探测突跳事件；对首次最强突跳的减速率恢复过程进行指数衰减拟合，并在涡旋蠕变（vortex-creep）理论框架下，采用贝叶斯推断方法对突跳后的ν̇演化进行建模，从而约束超流体转动惯量占比。

Result: 检测到三次突跳：最强一次发生在MJD 58352，分数频率变化约为2.7×10⁻⁶，且伴有约100天的指数恢复时间尺度和约0.015的小恢复分数，同时自转减速率幅度永久增大；另外两次较小突跳分别发生在MJD 59443和MJD 60445。贝叶斯建模得出超流体转动惯量分数约0.0187，与内壳层超流体理论预期一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果强化了标准超流体突跳范式，并表明即使此前无明显突跳活动的"安静"脉冲星，也可能蕴含显著的突跳活动和内部超流体动力学过程。

Abstract: We present the discovery and analysis of three rotational glitches in the young pulsar PSR J1637$-$4642. The timing observations span from 19 February 2009 to 6 October 2024 (MJD 54881$-$60589) from the Murriyang radio telescope of the Parkes Observatory. The first and strongest glitch occurred around MJD 58352 with a fractional frequency change of $Δν/ν\sim 2.7 \times 10^{-6}$, while two additional smaller glitches were detected at MJD 59443 and MJD 60445 with fractional changes of $2.2 \times 10^{-9}$ and $2.8 \times 10^{-8}$, respectively. Prior to this, the pulsar had shown no glitch activity since its discovery in the Parkes Multibeam survey. Only the first glitch exhibits detectable exponential recovery, with a decay timescale of $\sim$100 days and a small recovery fraction $\approx 0.015$, accompanied by a permanent increase in the magnitude of the spin-down rate. Modeling the post-glitch evolution of $\dotν$ within the vortex-creep framework using Bayesian inference gives a superfluid moment-of-inertia fraction $\approx 0.0187$, consistent with the inner-crust superfluid. These results reinforce the standard superfluid glitch paradigm and demonstrate that even ``quiet'' pulsars can still host substantial glitch activity.

</details>


### [92] [First Observational Evidence for QPO-like Coevolution between Characteristic Damping Timescales and X-ray Time Lags among AGNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19610)
*Haoyang Zhang,Li Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对窄线Seyfert 1星系RE J1034+396的多历元XMM-Newton观测进行了分析，利用阻尼随机游走过程分别拟合软带和硬带光变曲线，发现硬带特征阻尼时标与X射线时间延迟之间存在逆时针闭合环路，与先前报道的QPO频率-时间延迟环路高度相似，为AGN中热内流/冕的空间尺度变化同时影响QPO和随机变率提供了首个观测支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对RE J1034+396的多历元研究表明QPO频率与X射线时间延迟之间存在共同演化，但随机变率是否参与相同的结构演化尚不明确。作者旨在探究随机变率（以特征阻尼时标表征）是否与QPO和时间延迟一样，反映内吸积流/冕的循环结构演化。

Method: 对RE J1034+396的17个可靠观测历元，分别对软（0.3-1 keV）和硬（1-4 keV）两个能带的光变曲线采用阻尼随机游走（DRW）过程建模，提取特征阻尼时标（CDT），并与已有X射线时间延迟数据进行联合分析，比较CDT-时间延迟平面中的轨迹是否与QPO频率-时间延迟环路一致。

Result: 17个观测中软带CDT始终长于硬带CDT。硬带CDT与时间延迟共同构成逆时针闭合环路，形态与QPO频率-时间延迟环路高度相似；软带CDT则呈现更复杂的轨迹。在动力学、热学和黏滞的物理解释下，硬带CDT对应内热吸积流/冕内的特征尺度。

Conclusion: 观测到的环路可能代表内吸积流/冕共同循环演化的不同投影，三种时间可观测量的不同响应时标分别反映其动力学、随机性及辐射性质。这为AGN中热内流/冕的空间范围变化同时影响QPO与随机变率的物理图像提供了首个观测证据。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) and stochastic variability provide complementary probes of the inner accretion flow around supermassive black holes in active galactic nuclei (AGNs). Previous multi-epoch studies of narrow-line Seyfert 1 galaxy RE~J1034+396 revealed a coevolution between the QPO frequency and the X-ray time lag, but whether the stochastic variability participates in the same structural evolution has remained unclear. We analyze the multi-epoch XMM-Newton observations of RE~J1034+396 and model the soft (0.3--1~keV) and hard (1--4~keV) light curves separately using a damped random walk process. We obtain reliable characteristic damping timescales (CDTs) for 17 observations, with the soft-band CDT consistently longer than its hard-band counterpart. When combined with the X-ray time lag, the hard-band CDT traces a counterclockwise closed loop that closely resembles the previously reported QPO-frequency--time-lag loop, whereas the soft-band CDT exhibits more complex trajectory. Under plausible dynamical, thermal, and viscous interpretations, the hard-band CDT is associated with characteristic scales in the inner hot accretion flow/corona. The observed loops may represent different projections of a common cyclic evolution of the inner accretion flow/corona, as the three timing observables may respond to its dynamical, stochastic, and radiative properties on different timescales. These results provide the first observational support among AGNs for the physical picture in which changes in the spatial extent of the hot inner flow/corona simultaneously affect QPO and stochastic variability.

</details>


### [93] [Multi-scale Memory and Regime Shift in the Hyperactive Repeating FRB 20240114A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19713)
*We-Long Zhang,Sheng-Lun Xie,Jun-Jie Wei,Di Xiao,Long-Xuan Zhang,Shuang-Xi Yi,Fa-Yin Wang,Xue-Feng Wu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于FAST望远镜在214天内探测到的11,553个快速射电暴（FRB 20240114A）进行了统计分析，发现该源表现出多尺度记忆框架：短期随机行为、小时尺度上的相关活动（最长可达3600秒）以及数月尺度的系统性非平稳性，为爆发模型提供了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超活跃重复快速射电暴（FRB）的统计特性与时间记忆效应，揭示其爆发统计是否随观测基线变化，以及是否存在自组织临界性特征，为爆发物理模型提供观测约束。

Method: 基于FAST望远镜214天内探测到的11,553个FRB 20240114A爆发事件，采用事件率相干分析、等待时间分布拟合（多指数与阈值幂律）、重标度极差（R/S）分析计算Hurst指数、以及能量分布与等待时间分组的联合统计分析。

Result: 发现四项主要结果：(1) 最活跃日存在持续至3600秒的相关活动，为迄今报道最长；(2) 单日等待时间服从三指数分布，而214天全样本呈现阈值幂律尾部，表明统计特性依赖观测基线；(3) R/S分析显示等待时间呈破缺幂律，Hurst指数H1=0.63（短滞后弱记忆）与H2=1.04（长滞后非平稳漂移），断点约1小时，能量分析类似；(4) 能量分布斜率随等待时间变化但高能截断恒定，表明最大能量尺度为源固有属性。

Conclusion: FRB 20240114A展现多尺度记忆框架：短时标随机、小时尺度持续相关、数月尺度系统性非平稳，暗示自组织临界性特征；强调长期高节奏监测对理解FRB时间复杂性的必要性，为爆发模型提供关键基准。

Abstract: We present a statistical analysis of FRB~20240114A, a hyperactive repeating fast radio burst, based on 11,553 bursts detected by FAST over 214 days. Our main findings are fourfold. (1) On the most active day (MJD~60381, 3,197 bursts in 4.38 hr), event-rate coherence analysis reveals persistent correlated activity extending up to 3600~s, the longest reported for any repeating FRB, showing memory persists even in intense bursting epochs. (2) The waiting-time distribution on this day is well described by three exponentials, whereas the full 214-day sample develops a threshold power-law tail, indicating burst statistics depend on the observational baseline, with long-range correlations emerging only over longer timescales, a hallmark of self-organized criticality. (3) Rescaled range (R/S) analysis of waiting times reveals a broken power law, with Hurst exponents $H_1=0.63\pm0.02$ (short-lag weak memory) and $H_2=1.04\pm0.02$ (long-lag non-stationary drift). The break corresponds to $\sim$1 hour, consistent with the 3600~s coherence limit. R/S analysis of energies similarly exhibits a break ($H_1=0.60\pm0.01$, $H_2=1.10\pm0.05$) at a different lag, reinforcing that non-stationarity affects both temporal and energetic properties. (4) Energy distributions exhibit waiting-time-dependent slopes that are consistent with the full and daily samples, and the high-energy cutoff remains constant across waiting-time groups, suggesting that the maximum energy scale is an intrinsic source property. Together, these results establish a multi-scale memory framework: the source behaves stochastically on short timescales but exhibits systemic non-stationarity over months, providing benchmarks for burst models and highlighting the need for long-term, high-cadence monitoring to capture temporal complexity.

</details>


### [94] [Quasi-periodic Eruptions from Recurrent Satellite Black Hole Transits through Magnetized Galactic Nucleus Accretion Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19796)
*Na Wang,Jing-Tong Xing,Tong Liu,Ya-Ping Li,Shuang-Liang Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双通道模型来解释类周期爆发（QPEs）及其延迟紫外对应体：卫星黑洞（sBH）反复穿越核吸积盘，一方面通过引力聚焦和动力学阻力产生热光学厚抛射物，驱动软X射线QPE；另一方面压缩和弯曲磁场触发盘内磁重联，经光子扩散后产生延迟的更宽的紫外响应。该模型能同时解释Ansky的X射线与紫外观测，并解释了其他源缺乏紫外对应体的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类周期爆发（QPEs）是星系核中反复出现的软X射线耀发，但其物理起源尚不确定。Ansky源中探测到的延迟紫外（UV）对应体为限制可行的理论模型提供了新的约束，因此需要一个能够同时解释X射线和延迟UV信号的统一框架。

Method: 作者构建了双通道模型：(1) X射线通道：卫星黑洞反复穿越由大尺度磁场贯穿的核吸积盘，通过引力聚焦和动力学阻力产生热光学厚抛射物，其膨胀和光子扩散产生软X射线QPE；(2) UV通道：sBH运动压缩和弯曲背景磁场，触发盘内磁重联，耗散能量经光子扩散后形成延迟的更宽的紫外响应。

Result: 对于Bondi尺度的基准参数，模型给出特征X射线持续时间约10^3秒、光度约10^42 erg/s；在较低轨道倾角下，持续时间可延长到Ansky观测到的天级尺度。模型产生的热功率可与Ansky的可变紫外光度相比较。不利的磁场配置或长于QPE复发周期的扩散时间会削弱或抹平UV信号，这可解释其他QPE源缺乏清晰UV对应体的现象。

Conclusion: 该双通道模型能够统一解释QPE的软X射线耀发及其延迟紫外对应体，为Ansky的观测提供了自洽的理论框架，同时其预测的自然散落性解释了不同QPE源中UV信号存在与否的差异。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic eruptions (QPEs) are recurrent soft X-ray flares from galactic nuclei, but their origin remains uncertain. The delayed ultraviolet (UV) counterpart detected in Ansky provides a new constraint on viable models. We present a two-channel model in which a satellite black hole (sBH) repeatedly crosses a nuclear accretion disk threaded by a large-scale magnetic field. Gravitational focusing and dynamical drag generate hot, optically thick ejecta whose expansion and photon diffusion power the soft X-ray QPE. For fiducial Bondi-scale parameters, the model yields a characteristic X-ray duration of $\sim10^3\ \mathrm{s}$ and luminosity of $\sim10^{42}\ \mathrm{erg\,s^{-1}}$; at lower orbital inclinations, the duration extends to the day-long scale observed in Ansky. Simultaneously, the sBH motion compresses and bends the background magnetic field, triggering in-disk reconnection. The dissipated energy then emerges after photon diffusion as a broader, delayed UV response. The resulting thermal power is comparable to the variable UV luminosity of Ansky. Unfavorable magnetic fields or diffusion times longer than the QPE recurrence period can weaken or smear out the UV signal, potentially explaining the lack of clear UV counterparts in other QPE sources.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文报告了J0011+3443（TXS 0008+344，z=0.89）在2.3、4.9、8.5和23.6 GHz（首次）的多频VLBA观测结果。源由两个间隔314 pc的致密成分A和B以及一个在23.6 GHz下探测到的第三特征C组成。综合频谱呈GHz峰值（GPS）特征。VLBA成分频谱被解释为致密发射，低表面亮度发射被分辨掉。作者倾向于将该源解释为GPS类的致密对称天体（CSO），可能处于短暂或残余阶段，但也无法排除双AGN起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过多频高分辨率VLBA观测，表征J0011+3443的致密射电结构、频谱特性及其物理性质，以确定该源属于哪类射电天体（如GPS、CSO或双AGN），并澄清其演化状态。

Method: 采用VLBA在2.3、4.9、8.5和23.6 GHz四个频段进行高分辨率干涉观测，结合档案低分辨率射电数据构建综合频谱，并通过VLBA与低分辨率流量密度比值评估分辨损失，进而对致密成分进行频谱分析。

Result: 探测到两个间距约314 pc的致密成分A和B以及23.6 GHz下的第三特征C；综合频谱具有GHz峰值特征（峰频0.73 GHz，峰值流量879 mJy）；VLBA恢复的流量比例随频率下降（4.85 GHz为0.85，8.46 GHz为0.50），表明低表面亮度发射被分辨掉。

Conclusion: 综合形态学、频谱特性和各成分的致密谱特征，J0011+3443更可能是一个GPS类的致密对称天体，可能处于短暂或残余阶段；但双AGN解释需要多历元天测数据来最终排除。

Abstract: We present new multi-frequency VLBA observations of \object{J0011+3443} (TXS\,0008+344, $z=0.89$) at 2.3, 4.9, 8.5, and, for the first time, 23.6\,GHz. The source consists of two compact components A and B at a projected separation of $314\pm2$\,pc, plus a third feature C detected at 23.6\,GHz at $0.6$\,mas from A. Archival low-resolution radio measurements confirm an integrated gigahertz-peaked spectrum, with an observed-frame peak frequency of $ν_\mathrm{peak}=0.73\pm0.08$\,GHz and a peak flux density of $S_\mathrm{peak}=879\pm125$\,mJy. Comparison with nearly frequency-matched low-resolution measurements shows that the VLBA recovers $0.85\pm0.11$ of the 4.85\,GHz flux density and $0.50\pm0.06$ of the 8.46\,GHz flux density. The lower recovered fraction at 8.5\,GHz suggests that low-surface-brightness emission is resolved out or falls below the VLBA surface-brightness sensitivity. We therefore interpret the VLBA component spectra as spectra of the compact recovered emission only. The 23.6\,GHz morphology, the absence of a detected flat-spectrum core, the similar compact spectra of A and B, and the steep integrated GHz spectrum favor an interpretation of \object{J0011+3443} as a GPS-class compact symmetric object, possibly in a short-lived or relic phase, although a dual-AGN origin cannot be excluded without multi-epoch astrometry.

</details>


### [96] [Probabilistic kilonova prediction from gravitational wave inferred binary neutron star parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20262)
*Xiao-Fei Dong,Ik Siong Heng,Gavin P. Lamb,Chris Messenger,Nikhil Sarin,Benjamin Rayson,Nial R. Tanvir,Jessica Irwin,Thomas Wallace,Skye Rosetti,Bo Milvang-Jensen,Andrew J. Levan,Jens Hjorth*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出Genova，一个基于条件归一化流的概率框架，可直接从双中子星并合的引力波后验样本预测千新星光谱和光变曲线，并将模型参数边缘化以传播预测不确定性。应用于GW170817/AT2017gfo，预测区间能覆盖观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 千新星是研究中子星并合和r过程核合成的关键电磁窗口，但迄今为止仅探测到少数候选体，且只有AT2017gfo与引力波事件GW170817对应。需要一种能直接将引力波后验信息转化为千新星观测预测的工具，以更充分利用引力波数据。

Method: 使用条件归一化流学习静止系光谱在源系分量质量、潮汐形变率、视角和并合后时间条件下的分布。训练时将其他模型参数（如抛射物不透明度）边缘化，使其效应作为预测不确定性传播到最终预测中。采用自洽性测试验证模型，并重新发布了VISTA望远镜的Y、J、Ks波段重新校正光度数据。

Result: 自洽性测试中，流量模型复现中位光变曲线残差通常低于约0.1星等，预测中心68%区间宽度比主要在0.8-1.4之间（约0.4-8.0天）。与物理不同的千新星模型比较表明概率预测可推广。应用于GW170817/AT2017gfo时，预测区间能广泛覆盖观测值。

Conclusion: Genova成功建立了从引力波后验样本到千新星光谱和光变曲线的概率映射框架，能在保留引力波后验不确定性和边缘化模型参数不确定性的同时做出有信息量的预测，为未来双中子星并合事件的多信使观测提供重要工具。

Abstract: Kilonovae provide a key electromagnetic window into binary neutron star mergers, revealing the properties of the merging system while probing r-process nucleosynthesis of the Universe. However, only a few kilonova candidates have been detected to date, with AT2017gfo being the only one associated with a gravitational wave event, GW170817. In this work, we present \textsc{Genova}, a probabilistic framework for predicting kilonova spectra and light curves directly from gravitational wave posterior samples of binary neutron star mergers. This method uses a conditional normalising flow to learn the distribution of rest-frame spectra conditioned on the source-frame component masses, tidal deformabilities, viewing angle and time since merger. Other kilonova model parameters, such as ejecta opacities, are marginalised over during training, so that their effects are propagated into the predicted spectra as predictive uncertainty. In the self-consistency test, the flow model reproduces the median light curves with residuals typically below $\sim 0.1$ mag, and the ratio of the predicted central 68\% interval widths remains predominantly between $0.8$ and $1.4$ over $\sim 0.4$--$8.0$ days. Comparisons with a physically distinct kilonova model show that the probabilistic prediction can remain informative beyond the model used for training. We apply \textsc{Genova} to GW170817/AT2017gfo using multi-band observations, including newly re-reduced $Y$-, $J$-, $K_s$-band photometry from the Visible and Infrared Survey Telescope for Astronomy, which we present in this work. The resulting predictive intervals broadly encompass the observations while capturing both gravitational wave posterior uncertainty and the variation induced by marginalised kilonova model parameters.

</details>

### [97] [Observation of an excess at the top quark pair production threshold in the single-lepton channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19546)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验利用138 fb⁻¹的质子-质子对撞数据，在单轻子（电子或muon）+喷注末态中搜索顶夸克-反顶夸克准束缚态“toponium”。通过分析顶夸克对相对速度及两个角观测量（对自旋和宇称敏感），在t̄t产生阈值附近观察到超出标准模型NNLO QCD预言的显著事例数，对应总截面5.1±0.9 pb，与基于非相对论QCD的颜色单态赝标量toponium简化模型一致，观测显著性为6.1个标准差，独立证实了双轻子道中此前报告的超出信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准模型的强相互作用框架下，顶夸克质量极大（接近电弱对称破缺能标），其衰变宽度很宽而寿命极短，理论上在t̄t阈值附近可能形成由强力束缚的准束缚态（toponium）。由于顶夸克寿命短于束缚态形成时间，toponium信号非常微弱且与t̄t连续谱背景高度重叠。此前CMS在双轻子道报告了可能的超出信号，因此需要在其他衰变道（单轻子道）进行独立验证，以确认该信号的真实性并测量其性质。

Method: 利用CMS探测器在√s=13 TeV收集的138 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，选择单电子或单muon加多喷注的末态。通过三个关键观测量区分toponium信号与标准模型t̄t背景：(1) 顶夸克与反顶夸克之间的相对速度；(2) 两个对t̄t系统的自旋和宇称敏感的角度观测量。信号模型采用基于非相对论QCD（NRQCD）导出的颜色单态赝标量toponium简化模型，背景t̄t产生在NNLO精度的微扰QCD框架内计算。

Result: 观察到相对于标准模型NNLO t̄t产生预言的事例数显著超出，对应观测总截面为5.1±0.9 pb，与颜色单态赝标量toponium简化模型一致。观测显著性达到6.1个标准差，在单轻子道独立确认了双轻子道此前报告的超出信号。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了toponium准束缚态存在的独立证据，显著性为6.1σ，与基于NRQCD的颜色单态赝标量toponium模型相符。这为研究QCD束缚态动力学和顶夸克性质提供了新的实验途径，但仍需进一步数据以确认并更精确地测量该信号的性质。

Abstract: A search is presented for top quark-antiquark ($\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$) quasi-bound ``toponium'' states, near the kinematic $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ production threshold in final states with a single electron or muon and jets. The study uses proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, collected by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The analysis examines the relative velocity between the top quark and antiquark, along with two angular observables sensitive to the parity and spin of the $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ system. A significant excess of events is observed relative to the standard model prediction for $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ production calculated at next-to-next-to-leading order in perturbative quantum chromodynamics (QCD). The excess corresponds to an observed total cross section of 5.1$\pm$0.9 pb and is consistent with a simplified model of a color singlet pseudoscalar toponium motivated by nonrelativistic QCD, with a resulting observed significance of 6.1 standard deviations. The result provides an independent confirmation of the excess reported in the dilepton channel.

</details>


### [98] [Search for top quark flavor-changing neutral currents in multilepton final states using an effective field theory approach in proton--proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19547)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了在CMS探测器中利用13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度138 fb⁻¹）对顶夸克味改变中性流（FCNC）相互作用的首次全局搜索。这是首次在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下将顶夸克FCNC扇区所有相关算子同时约束在单一拟合中，使用双同号轻子或三轻子末态，通过多变量判别器和似然拟合对24个Wilson系数给出95%置信区间。未观察到FCNC相互作用证据，结果对FCNC顶夸克衰变分支比给出了迄今最严格的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶夸克的FCNC相互作用在标准模型中被强烈压制（分支比极低），因此任何观测到的信号都将是超出标准模型新物理的明确证据。此前对顶夸克FCNC的研究通常是针对单一算子或单一过程分别进行，缺乏在统一有效场论框架下对所有相关算子同时约束的全局分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过同时约束完整算子集来提供更可靠、更全面的FCNC限制。

Method: 该分析使用CMS探测器在质心能量√s=13 TeV下收集的质子-质子碰撞数据，积分亮度为138 fb⁻¹。信号在双同号轻子或三轻子末态中进行探测。采用多变量判别器将信号与背景分离，并利用这些判别器的分布进行似然拟合，以确定24个Wilson系数的95%置信区间。整个拟合在标准模型有效场论框架下同时约束所有相关算子。

Result: 未观察到顶夸克FCNC相互作用的证据。该分析首次对完整算子集提供了同时约束，并通过双夸克-双轻子相互作用对FCNC顶夸克衰变分支比给出了迄今为止最严格的限制。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了LHC上顶夸克FCNC领域的首个全局搜索框架，展示了在SMEFT框架下同时约束所有相关算子的可行性，为未来LHC的测量和组合分析提供了方法论基础。结果表明在此数据量下没有超出标准模型的新物理迹象，但显著收紧了顶夸克FCNC过程的参数空间。

Abstract: We present a search for flavor-changing neutral current (FCNC) interactions involving the top quark in production and decay. This analysis establishes the first global search in the top quark FCNC sector within the standard model effective field theory where all relevant operators are constrained simultaneously in a single fit to data, providing a framework for future measurements and combinations at the LHC. The analysis uses proton$-$proton collision data collected at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV by the CMS detector, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. Signal contributions are probed in final states with two same-sign charged leptons or three leptons. Multivariate discriminants are used to separate signal from background, and their distributions enter a likelihood fit to determine 95% confidence intervals for 24 Wilson coefficients. No evidence for FCNC interactions is observed. These results provide the first simultaneous constraints on the complete operator set and place the most stringent limits to date on FCNC top quark decay branching fractions via two-quark$-$two-lepton interactions.

</details>


### [99] [Heavy-flavour production and correlations in pp collisions: precision tests of pQCD and hadronisation with ALICE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19714)
*Deependra Sharma,for the ALICE Collaboration*

TL;DR: 本文报告了重夸克（粲和底）相关测量的初步结果与最终结果，包括pp碰撞中D介子产生的初步测量、B⁰介子产生截面（低至p_T=1 GeV/c）的最终结果、B介子产生的快度依赖性（与LHCb前向快度测量对比），以及D⁰–J/ψ联合产生的测量，所有结果均与pQCD计算和唯象模型比较，用于约束重夸克产生、强子化和多部分子相互作用动力学。


<details>

Conclusion: 这些测量为重夸克产生、强子化以及多部分子相互作用动力学提供了关键约束，有助于深入理解SPS与DPS过程的区分。

Abstract: Heavy quarks (charm and beauty) are predominantly produced in hard partonic scatterings, making their cross sections in proton--proton (pp) collisions calculable in perturbative quantum chromodynamics (pQCD) and thus providing stringent tests of pQCD. Furthermore, the associated production of two charm hadrons in a single collision offers a sensitive probe of multiparton interaction dynamics, distinguishing between single parton scattering (SPS) and double parton scattering (DPS) processes.
  In this contribution, preliminary measurements of prompt D-meson production are reported, together with the final results of the $\rm B^{0}$-meson production cross section down to $p_{\rm T}$ = 1 GeV/$c$ at midrapidity. The rapidity dependence of B-meson production is investigated by computing the ratio with respect to LHCb measurements at forward rapidity. The associated production of $\rm D^{0}$--$\mathrm{J}/ψ$ pairs in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV is presented as well, where $\rm D^{0}$ mesons are reconstructed at midrapidity, while $\mathrm{J}/ψ$ candidates are measured at forward rapidity. These measurements are compared with pQCD calculations and phenomenological models, providing crucial constraints on heavy-quark production, hadronisation, and multiparton interaction dynamics.

</details>


### [100] [The Search Budget of the BSM Resonance Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19764)
*Theresa Reisch,Tobias Golling*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文定量分析了ATLAS超出标准模型（BSM）共振态搜寻项目中的"look-elsewhere效应"（多重检验惩罚）。基于公开数据，作者统计了现有104个能谱扫描对应的有效独立分辨率元，得出试验因子N_trials=7.9×10³，对应的5σ全局显著性需局部Z=6.55。若对最多四个重建粒子构成的所有质量进行完全组合扫描（一次一个事件级选择，并结合Run 2和Run 3数据），则试验因子增至3.6×10⁵，所需局部显著性提升至Z=7.11——即增加了46倍试验次数，仅付出了0.56σ的显著性代价。对于机器学习类搜寻（估计器不完美、无法枚举试验因子），两阶段解盲是防止虚假信号的有效保障，且在一个已发表的bump-hunting网络上测量的缺陷率下，该方法具有更高的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中，数据导向扫描、异常搜寻和一般性搜寻会同时检验大量质量谱，其中look-elsewhere效应决定了发现判据的门槛。然而，传统上人们混淆"试验次数"与"试验因子"的概念，且缺乏对现有搜寻计划与潜在完全组合扫描之间代价的系统量化。作者旨在用公开数据量化ATLAS BSM共振搜寻的试验因子，并评估完全组合扫描所需的显著性代价，同时为机器学习搜寻中无法枚举试验因子的困境提供解盲策略的替代方案。

Method: 作者首先定义了"试验因子"与"试验次数"之间的区别，将试验因子确定为物理上独立的分辨率元数量。他们仅从公开输入数据出发，在有效独立分辨率元的维度上统计ATLAS BSM共振程序的试验因子，累计覆盖104个能谱。随后，他们设想并计算了完全组合扫描的代价：即由最多四个重建对象构成的所有质量组合，一次采用一种事件级选择，并对Run 2和Run 3数据联合定价。此外，对于机器学习搜寻中试验因子不可枚举的情况，作者提出两阶段解盲作为防护机制，并通过已发布的bump-hunting网络的缺陷率数据比较其敏感性。

Result: ATLAS公布的104个共振搜寻谱合计对应N_trials=7.9×10³次有效独立观测，5σ全局发现需要局部Z=6.55。完全组合扫描（每类事件选择一次，联合Run 2和Run 3）对应3.6×10⁵次looks，需Z_local=7.11。这意味着试验次数增加46倍仅使局部显著性门槛提高0.56σ。在机器学习搜寻情景下，基于已发布bump-hunting网络的实测缺陷率，两阶段解盲被证明是更敏感的保护方案。

Conclusion: 该研究首次用公开数据系统量化了ATLAS BSM共振搜寻的多个试验因子，并证明从现有扫描扩展至完全组合扫描的边际显著性代价相对温和（增加46倍试验仅多需0.56σ）。这表明涵盖所有可能共振组合的"充分"扫描在实际显著性要求上是可行且值得探索的。对于机器学习驱动的搜寻，当无法枚举试验因子时，两阶段解盲提供了理论上可保证的保护措施，且在实测缺陷率下其敏感性优于备选方案。

Abstract: Data-directed scans, anomaly searches and general searches probe many mass spectra at once, and for them the look-elsewhere effect sets the discovery bar. We count the trials factor of the ATLAS BSM resonance program in effective independent resolution elements, from public inputs alone, and then work out what a fully combinatorial scan would cost in the same units. The published record, summed over the 104 spectra it scans, amounts to $N_{\mathrm{trials}} = 7.9 \times 10^{3}$ looks, so a $5σ$ global discovery today costs a local $Z_{\mathrm{local}} = 6.55$. Scanning instead every mass built from up to four reconstructed objects, one event-level selection at a time and priced on Run 2 and Run 3 together, amounts to $3.6 \times 10^{5}$ looks and $Z_{\mathrm{local}} = 7.11$. A factor 46 more looks therefore costs $0.56σ$. If the trials factor cannot be enumerated because the estimator is itself imperfect, as in machine-learning searches, two-stage unblinding is a safeguard against its spurious signals: at the defect rate measured for a published bump-hunting network it is the more sensitive procedure.

</details>


### [101] [Distilling Normalizing Flows for Real-Time Anomaly Detection at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19912)
*Tara P. A. Tahseen,Noah Clarke Hall,Nikolaos Konstantinidis,Paula Martínez Suárez*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出将大规模归一化流的似然度蒸馏到轻量级学生模型（决策树或量化神经网络）中，以在FPGA硬件触发器中实现快速异常检测，同时解决缺失输入特征下的精确似然估计问题，兼顾物理性能与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化流作为异常检测器具有原理稳健的优势，但其逐事件似然计算的极高延迟和资源消耗使其无法部署在大强子对撞机（LHC）的硬件触发器中。需要通过蒸馏等方式降低推理成本，使流式似然估计能在硬件约束下实用化。

Method: 将大型归一化流学到的似然函数蒸馏为适合FPGA部署的轻量级学生估计器。学生模型分两类：决策树和神经网络（后者采用先进量化技术优化）。同时使用条件归一化流以支持在缺失输入特征的情况下进行精确似然估计。

Result: 蒸馏方法同时提升了似然质量并降低了推理成本，在物理性能和延迟两方面均优于现有基于流的方法，达到了最先进水平（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: 通过知识蒸馏将归一化流的似然估计能力迁移到硬件友好的轻量模型，成功突破了硬件触发的延迟和资源瓶颈，证明了在极端约束条件下部署高质量概率异常检测的可行性。

Abstract: Normalizing flows are principled anomaly detectors, selecting anomalies using a probabilistic per-event likelihood. Extreme latency and resource constraints have prevented the deployment of flow likelihoods within the hardware triggers at the Large Hadron Collider. We bypass these limitations by distilling the likelihood from a large normalizing flow into lightweight student estimators suitable for deployment on a field-programmable gate array. Our use of a conditional normalizing flow enables precise likelihood estimation in the presence of missing input features. Both decision tree and neural network students are considered, the latter optimized under advanced quantization techniques. By simultaneously improving likelihood quality and lowering inference cost, we demonstrate both state-of-the-art physics performance and latency compared with existing flow-based approaches.

</details>

### [102] [A fully discrete LBRFD-IPDG method for linear fourth-order parabolic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19250)
*Hongying Huang,Hong Zhang,Yanming Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种求解带Dirichlet边界条件线性四阶抛物方程的完全离散方法，在时间方向采用隐式LBRFD多步格式，空间方向采用混合内罚间断Galerkin（IPDG）方法。通过辅助变量重构空间离散，LBRFD方法在特定参数对下具有A(α)-稳定性且稳定角优于同阶BDFp方法。理论分析给出h^(k-1)+τ^p的L2误差估计，数值实验证实稳定性并展示h^(k+1)+τ^p的最优收敛阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性四阶抛物方程在物理和工程中具有广泛应用（如薄膜、梁振动等），需要构造高效稳定的数值方法。传统方法在稳定性、收敛阶或边界处理方面存在局限，因此需要发展结合高效时间步进与灵活空间离散的完全离散格式，并给出严格的稳定性与误差分析。

Method: 时间方向采用等距线性重心有理插值的隐式LBRFD多步格式，包含启动程序；空间方向引入辅助变量重构原始问题，采用混合内罚间断Galerkin（IPDG）方法进行离散。通过G-能量技术和离散Grönwall引理建立稳定性与先验误差估计。LBRFD格式的A(α)-稳定性通过参数对(n,d)的选择来分析，并对比同阶BDFp方法。

Result: 理论分析得到L2误差估计为h^(k-1)+τ^p（p=d若n-d为偶数，p=d+1若n-d为奇数），空间收敛阶的降低归因于边界∂Ω上的贡献。数值实验验证了方法的稳定性，且实际观测到h^(k+1)+τ^p的最优收敛阶，优于理论预测。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种理论上稳定且数值上表现出更优收敛性的四阶抛物方程求解格式。LBRFD时间离散相比同阶BDFp具有更宽的稳定角，IPDG空间离散结合辅助变量重构是有效的策略。理论与数值结果之间的收敛阶差异（h^(k+1) vs h^k-1）提示理论估计可能可以进一步优化。

Abstract: We propose a fully discrete method for linear fourth-order parabolic equations with Dirichlet boundary conditions, combining an implicit LBRFD multistep scheme in time with a mixed interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin (IPDG) method in space. The temporal discretization employs equispaced linear barycentric rational interpolants and incorporates a startup procedure. To facilitate the spatial discretization, the original problem is reformulated through an auxiliary variable. For certain parameter pairs $(n,d)$, the LBRFD method is shown to be $A(α)$-stable and to possess a wider stability angle than the corresponding BDF$p$ method of the same order. Stability and a priori error estimates are established via a $G$-energy technique and the discrete Grönwall lemma. The theoretical analysis yields a total $L^2$ error estimate of order $h^{k-1}+τ^p$, where $p=d$ if $n-d$ is even and $p=d+1$ if $n-d$ is odd. The reduced spatial convergence rate is attributed to boundary contributions on $\partialΩ$. Despite this theoretical prediction, numerical experiments confirm the stability and demonstrate optimal convergence of order $h^{k+1}+τ^p$.

</details>


### [103] [A High-Order Rank-Adaptive Implicit Algorithm for Solving High Dimensional Diffusion Equations using the Hierarchical Tucker Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19368)
*Paolo Bosques-Paulet*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分层Tucker（HT）分解的高阶秩自适应隐式积分器，用于求解高维扩散方程的张量解。该方法将原有三维Tucker分解方法推广到更高维度，通过二叉树的基与核张量表示来避免Tucker分解在高维下的存储爆炸问题，并结合谱方法（空间离散）与对角隐式Runge-Kutta方法（时间离散）实现高精度求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的3D Tucker分解方法在维度d>3时存储复杂度随维度指数增长，无法有效扩展到高维问题。需要一种新的张量格式和相应的时间积分策略，使得高维扩散方程的张量求解在存储和计算上可行。

Method: 采用分层Tucker（HT）分解替代Tucker分解，将解按二叉树结构分解为各维基张量与中间核张量。空间上使用谱方法离散，时间上使用对角隐式Runge-Kutta方法。在每个RK阶段，利用前序阶段计算的基张量进行增广，以预测下一阶段的基并构造投影子空间；通过向增广子空间投影，从叶到根顺序更新基和核张量。与3D Tucker方法仅含单一核张量不同，HT方法还需更新中间层次的核张量。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法具有高阶精度，并测试了在不同时变扩散系数下积分器对解秩的捕捉能力。

Conclusion: HT分解结合隐式Runge-Kutta积分器是高维扩散方程的一种有效张量求解方案，能够克服Tucker分解在高维下的存储瓶颈，同时保持高阶精度和秩自适应能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a high-order rank-adaptive implicit integrator for the tensor solution of high-dimensional diffusion equations. We extend the 3D version of this method from the Tucker decomposition to higher dimensions using the hierarchical Tucker (HT) decomposition, since the storage complexity for the Tucker decomposition increases exponentially with the number of dimensions $d>3$. The HT format avoids this issue by decomposing the solution according to a binary tree consisting of bases for each dimension and core tensors which connect the bases. Spectral methods are considered for spatial discretization, and diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta methods are considered for time discretization. At each stage of the Runge-Kutta method, the bases computed at the previous stages are augmented to predict the upcoming basis and construct projection subspaces. By projecting onto these enriched subspaces, the bases and cores can be updated in a sequential manner going up the tree from leaf-to-root. Unlike the 3D Tucker method which has a single core tensor, the HT method also updates the intermediate core tensors. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the method observes high-order accuracy, and test how well the integrator captures the solution rank for various sets of time-dependent diffusion coefficients.

</details>


### [104] [A high-order, meshless, Lagrangian--Eulerian RBF-FD method for advection--diffusion--reaction on moving manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19384)
*Matthew Lowery,Grady B. Wright,Varun Shankar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于三维空间中余维为一的移动流形上偏微分方程的高阶径向基函数生成有限差分（RBF-FD）方法。该方法结合拉格朗日与欧拉处理方式，利用切平面公式重构表面微分算子，并通过自适应重排、后向半拉格朗日回溯、超黏性稳定化、多种时间相干性更新策略以及基于表面求积的标量投影来分别处理标记漂移、多步历史重构、数值稳定、计算效率和质量守恒问题。数值实验展示了高阶收敛性、无源问题中的舍入级守恒、稳定的长时间积分以及显著的效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动流形上的PDE数值求解具有重要应用，但传统方法在处理表面演化、标记漂移、几何变化和长时间稳定性方面面临挑战。现有表面RBF-FD方法多针对静态流形，而移动流形需要同时处理几何演化与表面微分算子的重构，且需要兼顾高阶精度、质量守恒和计算效率。

Method: 方法建立在表面RBF-FD的切平面公式之上，采用混合拉格朗日-欧拉框架：流形及其物质导数以拉格朗日方式演化，而表面微分算子则在瞬时点云上重构。具体关键技术包括：(1) 通过紧凑全局参数模型进行自适应重排以处理拉格朗日标记漂移，并提供准确的法向量和基于几何的求积；(2) 重排后采用后向半拉格朗日回溯和插值重建多步历史；(3) 利用时间相干性的三种更新策略——局部RBF-FD权重的缺陷校正、基于曲率更新的超黏性系数（在谱重算之间）、以及复用ILU分解的全局缺陷校正迭代以加速预条件GMRES；(4) 通过基于演化表面求积的标量投影强制全局质量守恒。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法实现了高阶收敛，在无源问题中质量守恒达舍入精度水平，长时间积分稳定，且所提出的各种更新策略带来了可观的计算成本节省。

Conclusion: 该混合拉格朗日-欧拉RBF-FD方法为移动流形上的PDE提供了高效、稳定且高精度的求解框架，其多种时间相干性利用策略有效降低了计算负担，为后续在更复杂的表面动力学问题中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a high-order radial basis function-generated finite difference (RBF-FD) method for partial differential equations on moving manifolds $\mathcal M(t)\subset\mathbb R^3$ of co-dimension one. Our method builds on the tangent-plane formulation of surface RBF-FD and combines Lagrangian and Eulerian treatments: the manifold and material derivative are evolved in a Lagrangian fashion, while the remaining surface differential operators are reconstructed on the instantaneous point cloud and stabilized, when necessary, by a quasi-analytical hyperviscosity formulation. Lagrangian marker drift is handled by adaptive rearrangement using a compact global parametric model of the moving surface, which also supplies accurate normals and geometry-based quadrature. After rearrangement, we reconstruct the multistep history by backward semi-Lagrangian tracing and interpolation before resuming the Lagrangian time discretization. We exploit temporal coherence through three update strategies: defect correction for the local RBF-FD weights, a curvature-based update of the hyperviscosity coefficients between spectral recomputations, and a global defect-correction iteration that reuses an incomplete LU (ILU) factorization before preconditioned generalized minimal residual (GMRES) iterations. Finally, we enforce the prescribed global mass balance through a scalar projection based on the evolving surface quadrature. Numerical experiments demonstrate high-order convergence, conservation to roundoff in source-free problems, stable long-time integration, and substantial savings from the proposed update strategies.

</details>


### [105] [Implicit-adjoint finite-volume topology optimization of two-dimensional conjugate heat transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19426)
*Sam Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种透明、自包含的二维有限体积拓扑优化框架，用于共轭传热设计，基于交错MAC网格和Stokes-Brinkman/Darcy流动，采用精确离散伴随（隐函数定理）和严格的验收门控，确保可复现性和数值稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑高热效率热架构设计需在固体传导、流体对流和流阻之间权衡，密度法拓扑优化虽提供系统框架，但其可复现性和数值稳定性严重依赖底层离散求解器与伴随灵敏度机制，缺乏透明、可检验的参考实现。

Method: 采用交错Marker-and-Cell（MAC）网格上的二维有限体积离散；设计依赖的能量输运耦合Stokes-Brinkman/Darcy流，固定固体体积约束；构造满足u·∇T=∇·(uT)-T(∇·u)恒等式的离散对流算子以消除虚假热源；通过隐函数定理而非迭代展开计算逆模式导数，获得精确离散伴随；用制造解方法和方向泰勒余项检验验证算子；采用投影梯度+β连续化优化，并设置残差收敛、质量守恒、体积可行性和数值有限性的预声明门控。

Result: 通过制造解方法和方向泰勒余项测试系统验证了离散算子；在四个代表性热流体设计基准上成功优化，所有候选迭代均在满足严格验收门控后才被接受和公布，证明了框架的可复现性和确定性和数值稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可检查、确定性的参考堆栈，用于可验证的共轭热流体拓扑优化，弥补了该领域在透明性和可复现性上的不足。

Abstract: Designing compact, high-efficiency thermal architectures requires resolving the competing demands of solid conduction, fluid convection, and flow resistance within highly constrained physical envelopes. Density-based topology optimization provides a systematic framework for synthesizing these coupled layouts, yet the reproducibility and numerical stability of the resulting designs depend critically on the underlying discrete solvers and adjoint sensitivity mechanics. In this work, we present a transparent, self-contained two-dimensional finite-volume formulation on a staggered Marker-and-Cell grid for conjugate heat transfer governed by design-dependent energy transport coupled to Stokes--Brinkman or Darcy flow at fixed solid volume. To prevent spurious artificial thermal sources in porous, weakly compressible Brinkman domains, the discrete advection operator is constructed to satisfy the identity $\mathbf{u}\cdot\nabla T=\nabla\cdot(\mathbf{u}T)-T(\nabla\cdot\mathbf{u})$ cellwise, ensuring that uniform temperature fields remain exact discrete nullspaces even under inexact continuity satisfaction. Reverse-mode derivatives are evaluated via the implicit function theorem rather than unrolled iterative loops, yielding exact discrete adjoints with bounded memory requirements. The discrete operators are systematically validated through the method of manufactured solutions and directional Taylor remainder tests. Four representative thermofluid design benchmarks are optimized using a projected-gradient scheme with $β$-continuation, wherein candidate iterates are accepted and published only upon satisfying rigorous, predeclared gates on residual convergence, mass conservation, volume feasibility, and numerical finiteness. The resulting formulation provides an inspectable, deterministic reference stack for verifiable conjugate thermofluidic topology optimization.

</details>


### [106] [The Normal Procrustes Problem: A Riemannian Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19513)
*Kyle Bierly*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了求解Normal Procrustes问题（最小化||AX-Y||_F²，其中A为正规矩阵）的数值方法。通过将复数和实数目标函数约化到酉矩阵与实正交矩阵的黎曼流形上进行优化，该方法同时适用于Real Normal Procrustes问题，并作为特例涵盖Closest Normal Matrix问题。数值实验表明该方法在所有相关问题上均具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Normal Procrustes问题在文献中尚无求解方法，作者旨在填补这一空白。该问题与已有研究的Closest Normal Matrix问题密切相关，后者的求解方法已有先例，但在Procrustes框架下的推广尚未被探索。

Method: 将复数和实数的Normal Procrustes目标函数化简为仅依赖于酉矩阵（复数情形）或实正交矩阵（实数情形）的黎曼流形上的优化问题，从而运用黎曼流形优化技术（如最优化算法在该流形上的迭代）来近似求解。该方法自然覆盖Closest Normal Matrix问题的求解。

Result: 通过数值实验验证了该方法在所有相关问题上的性能：相比已有算法（针对Closest Normal Matrix问题），获得了具有竞争力的残差结果，并且在墙钟时间上具有更优的扩展性（scaling）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为Normal Procrustes问题及其实数版本提供了可行的数值求解方法，同时为Closest Normal Matrix问题提供了新的黎曼优化途径，在精度与计算效率上均有良好表现。

Abstract: For given $m \times n$ data matrices $X, Y$, we investigate the Normal Procrustes Problem---the least squares optimization problem that aims to minimize $\|AX-Y\|_F^2$, where $A$ is constrained to be a normal $m \times m$ matrix. As far as the author of this article is aware, no other method that attempts to solve the Normal Procrustes Problem exists in the literature; we thus propose what is, to our knowledge, the first such method. We, furthermore, adapt our approach to address the Real Normal Procrustes Problem, where $A$ must be real. In our treatment of these problems, we first reduce our complex and real objective functions to be purely optimizable over the Riemannian manifolds of the unitary and real orthogonal matrices, respectively. This reduction enables us to apply techniques in Riemannian manifold optimization to approximate solutions to both. The Closest Normal Matrix and Real Closest Normal Matrix Problems are both special cases of their respective Procrustes Problems and have been previously studied in the literature. Our approach thus recovers a novel Riemannian optimization method for approximating solutions to both these problems. We further numerically test the performance of our method across all such problems (including against previously developed algorithms on the Closest Normal Matrix Problems) and obtain competitive residuals and favorable scaling in wall-clock time.

</details>


### [107] [An adaptive and conservative low-rank IMEX solver for the hybrid ion Vlasov-Fokker-Planck and fluid electron system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19603)
*Joseph Nakao,Dylan T. Jacobs,William Taitano*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种宏观守恒、秩自适应的数值方法，用于求解混合Vlasov-Fokker-Planck（VFP）方程（离子动力学、电子流体），该方法将RAIL秩自适应、高阶隐式-显式时间推进和LoMaC保守截断等技术统一到同一框架中，并扩展到柱坐标系，有效解决维数灾难、多尺度刚性和结构保持等耦合挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合VFP系统求解面临多项耦合挑战：维数灾难、宏观量的守恒性、多尺度模型带来的刚性问题以及结构保持。现有方法各自只能解决单一困难，缺乏一个统一框架来同时应对所有这些挑战。

Method: 将三种既有方法整合到统一框架中：（1）采用Reduced Augmentation Implicit Low-rank (RAIL)方法进行速度空间的秩自适应以应对维数灾难；（2）采用高阶隐式-显式（IMEX）时间推进处理多尺度刚度；（3）采用Local Macroscopic Conservative (LoMaC)过程实现解的保守截断。此外将RAIL和LoMaC方法从笛卡尔坐标扩展到柱坐标，支持隐式秩自适应时间积分并利用结构保持离散。

Result: 在一组测试问题上验证了该方案的正确性和有效性，并通过模拟驻激波（standing shock）问题证明了宏观量守恒的重要性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架能够同时解决混合VFP方程求解中的维数灾难、宏观守恒、刚性和结构保持等挑战，且宏观量的守恒对物理结果的正确性至关重要。

Abstract: We present a macroscopically conservative, rank-adaptive method for solving a hybrid Vlasov-Fokker-Planck (VFP) equation in which the ions are treated kinetically, and the electrons are treated as a fluid. Solving this system poses several coupled computational challenges: the curse of dimensionality, conservation of macroscopic quantities, stiffness arising from the multiscale nature of the model, and structure preservation. To address these challenges, we combine several established methods, each targeting a specific difficulty, into a unified framework for solving this nonlinear system. Specifically, we employ the recent Reduced Augmentation Implicit Low-rank (RAIL) method for rank adaptivity in velocity space to combat the curse of dimensionality, high-order implicit-explicit time stepping to handle the multiscale stiffness, and the Local Macroscopic Conservative (LoMaC) procedure for conservative truncation of the solution. The RAIL and LoMaC methods are extended from Cartesian to cylindrical coordinates. The resulting framework accommodates implicit rank-adaptive time integration while remaining conservative and leveraging structure-preserving discretizations. We verify the scheme on a suite of test problems and simulate a standing shock to demonstrate the importance of conserving the macroscopic quantities.

</details>


### [108] [Evo-GTransNet for Parabolic PDEs: A Fixed-Feature Galerkin Method of Lines with Quadrature-Mass Orthonormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19615)
*Lili Ju,Jin Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种进化型广义可迁移神经网络（Evo-GTransNet）求解器，用于求解抛物型偏微分方程，采用保留空间-固定特征Galerkin方法线格式，通过二次加权截断SVD解决质量矩阵病态问题，并建立了半离散能量律和全离散误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的神经网络求解偏微分方程通常需要在时间积分过程中进行非线性训练，计算成本高且数值稳定性难以保证。同时，抛物型偏微分方程的离散化常面临质量矩阵严重病态的问题，影响了数值方法的精度和稳定性。

Method: 采用保留空间-固定特征Galerkin方法线格式：GTransNet提供预定义的空间字典，仅保留输出系数随时间演化，避免非线性训练。针对质量矩阵病态问题，采用二次加权截断SVD选取数值可分辨的试验空间，随后通过独立重标定使基关于装配-求积质量内积正交。时间离散采用隐式中点格式，并证明方法具有收缩性。

Result: 数值实验表明方法具有二阶时间收敛精度，能够量化严重原始质量病态条件下保留空间的精度。在高频和多尺度基准问题中，GTransNet在相同标称输出维度下，在测试的固定特征字典中取得了最小平均验证误差。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经网络求解抛物型偏微分方程提供了一种无需非线性训练的稳定数值框架，通过数学上严格的半离散能量律、收缩性和误差估计保证了方法的理论可靠性，并在典型基准问题上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop an evolutionary generalized transferable neural network (Evo-GTransNet) solver for parabolic partial differential equations, formulated as a retained-space fixed-feature Galerkin method of lines. A GTransNet provides the prescribed spatial dictionary, while only the retained output coefficients evolve, thereby avoiding nonlinear training during time integration. To address severe mass-matrix ill-conditioning, we apply a quadrature-weighted truncated singular value decomposition (SVD) to select the numerically resolved trial space, followed by a separate rescaling that makes its basis orthonormal with respect to the assembly-quadrature mass inner product. Rank truncation modifies the approximation space, whereas the subsequent orthonormalization changes only its coordinate representation and preserves the retained discrete functions in exact arithmetic. The resulting semidiscrete coefficient system has an identity mass matrix, and we establish a semidiscrete energy law for symmetric linear parabolic problems. With the implicit midpoint scheme for time discretization, we further prove the contractivity of the method and derive a conditional fully discrete error estimate in which the error is controlled by the retained-space approximation error and the consistency defects. Numerical experiments demonstrate second-order temporal convergence using repeated feature samples together with separate assembly and validation quadratures, and quantify the accuracy of the retained space in the presence of severe raw-mass ill-conditioning. For the high-frequency and multiscale benchmark problems considered here, GTransNet achieves the smallest mean validation errors among the tested fixed-feature dictionaries at the same nominal output dimension.

</details>


### [109] [Learning Deterministic and Stochastic Forced Hamiltonian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19688)
*Benedikt Brantner,Tomasz Tyranowski*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何框架的神经网络方法，用于学习确定性和随机强迫哈密顿系统。通过引入Lagrange-d'Alembert映射并证明C^r收敛定理，作者提出了广义强迫哈密顿神经网络（GFHNNs）及其参数化变体（PGFHNNs），并证明了其通用逼近定理。数值实验表明，该几何架构在长期稳定性和精度上显著优于非几何残差神经网络，且需要更少的训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络在模拟哈密顿系统时往往无法保持系统的几何结构（如辛结构），导致长期仿真中能量漂移和数值不稳定。作者希望利用变分积分器理论和Lagrange-d'Alembert原理，构建保持物理结构的学习框架，以提升长时程动力系统建模的准确性与稳定性。

Method: 首先受Lagrange-d'Alembert原理启发引入Lagrange-d'Alembert映射概念，对一阶一步方法建立C^r收敛定理。在此基础上，通过拼接Lagrange-d'Alembert-Euler映射构造GFHNNs，并证明其通用逼近定理。随后将框架扩展到参数依赖系统，提出PGFHNNs。对于随机强迫哈密顿系统，将Stratonovich-Taylor展开中的多重Stratonovich积分解释为参数，从而复用同一框架处理随机情形（前提是可获得维纳过程信息）。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的几何架构（GFHNNs/PGFHNNs）在长时程稳定性和精度上显著优于非几何残差神经网络，同时在达到同等性能水平时所需训练数据量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 基于几何原理构建的神经网络结构能够有效保持动力系统的物理结构，为确定性及随机强迫哈密顿系统的学习提供了理论上严谨且实践中高效的新框架。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for learning deterministic and stochastic forced Hamiltonian systems with neural networks. Motivated by the Lagrange-d'Alembert principle and the theory of variational integrators, we introduce the notion of a Lagrange-d'Alembert map and establish a $C^r$ convergence theorem for first-order one-step methods. Building on these results, we propose Generalized Forced Hamiltonian Neural Networks (GFHNNs), a class of structure-preserving neural networks obtained by concatenating Lagrange-d'Alembert-Euler maps, and prove a universal approximation theorem for this architecture. We further extend the framework to parameter-dependent systems, leading to Parametric Generalized Forced Hamiltonian Neural Networks (PGFHNNs). By interpreting the multiple Stratonovich integrals appearing in the Stratonovich-Taylor expansion as parameters, the same framework can be applied to stochastic forced Hamiltonian systems whenever information about the underlying Wiener process is available. Our numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed geometric architectures provide significantly improved long-time stability and accuracy compared to non-geometric residual neural networks, while requiring substantially less training data to achieve a comparable level of performance.

</details>


### [110] [Exact hierarchical algorithms for accelerating particle--mesh coupling in sparse-grid particle-in-cell methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19702)
*Clément Guillet*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏网格组合技术（SGCT-PIC）和分层稀疏网格（HSG-PIC）两类粒子网格方法，提出了两种基于快速多极子（FMM）思想的分层算法，用于电荷沉积和电场插值。算法利用粒子簇与有向无环图（DAG）关联，将粒子-网格交互的算术复杂度从O(p^d n^{d-1}N)降低到O(p^d(N+M))。由于使用分段多项式核函数，多极展开是精确的，无需截断或近似，且无需多极到局部展开的平移。二维数值实验显示电荷沉积和电场插值均获得数倍至数十倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SGCT-PIC和HSG-PIC方法中，电荷沉积和电场插值的计算复杂度随网格层级n和粒子数N呈超线性增长（O(p^d n^{d-1}N)），在高粒子每格（PPC）比率的应用中计算开销巨大。现有方法缺乏有效利用粒子空间聚类结构来降低粒子-网格交互次数的策略。

Method: 受快速多极子方法启发，构建粒子填充盒子的有向无环图，利用粒子簇减少粒子-网格交互次数。由于采用分段多项式核函数，多极展开完全精确，无需截断或近似，且近场和远场均有效，消除了多极到局部的平移操作。算法将复杂度从O(p^d n^{d-1}N)降至O(p^d(N+M))，其中M=2^{dn}为全网格节点数。

Result: 二维数值实验中，SGCT-PIC电荷沉积加速8.2倍-66.9倍，HSG-PIC加速3.1倍-18.8倍；电场插值分别加速4.1倍-62.6倍和4.2倍-13.7倍。加速比随粒子每格比率增大而增大，且保持精确的粒子-网格交互不变。

Conclusion: 提出的分层算法显著降低了SGCT-PIC和HSG-PIC中粒子-网格交互的计算复杂度，在大粒子群体和高峰值粒子密度的应用中优势尤为明显，同时保持交互的精确性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose two hierarchical algorithms for charge deposition and electric-field interpolation that apply to both the sparse-grid combination technique (SGCT-PIC) and hierarchical sparse-grid (HSG-PIC) particle-in-cell methods. The two algorithms are inspired by the fast multipole method (FMM) and exploit clusters of particles associated with a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of particle-populated boxes to reduce the number of particle--mesh interactions. The particle--mesh interactions are governed by piecewise-polynomial kernels, so that the associated multipole expansions are exact, requiring neither truncation nor approximation, and are valid in both near- and far-field regions, thereby eliminating the need for multipole-to-local translations. The arithmetic complexity of the charge deposition and field interpolation steps is reduced from $Ø(p^d n^{d-1}N)$ to $Ø(p^d(N+M))$, where $M=2^{dn}$ denotes the number of full-grid mesh nodes and is typically no larger than the particle population in the considered regime, $M\lesssim N$. Numerical experiments in two-dimensional configurations demonstrate charge-deposition speedups of $8.2\times$--$66.9\times$ for SGCT-PIC and $3.1\times$--$18.8\times$ for HSG-PIC, and field-interpolation speedups of $4.1\times$--$62.6\times$ and $4.2\times$--$13.7\times$, respectively, depending on the particle-per-cell ratio, while preserving the exact particle--mesh interactions. The speedups increase with the particle-per-cell ratio, reflecting the reduced dependence of the hierarchical algorithms on the number of particles and their increasing advantage for large particle populations.

</details>


### [111] [Optimal Sobolev Approximation by Deterministic and Random Shallow Sigmoidal Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19797)
*Zhaohui Fu,Yangshuai Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有标准光滑sigmoid激活的浅层网络，在一般维度下解决了其最优Sobolev逼近能力问题。对于包括tanh、logistic sigmoid和erf在内的一类光滑sigmoid激活函数（其导函数具有Schwartz类衰减），作者构建了确定性方向-偏移字典，使得任意u∈H^k(Ω)都能以M^{-(k-m)/d}的误差阶在H^m(Ω)中被逼近（0≤m≤k），该速率在Kolmogorov宽度意义下是最优的。进一步，作者证明了从任意有界非零密度分布独立采样参数得到的随机字典以高概率达到相同逼近指数（至多对数因子超采样）。数值实验验证了预测的代数速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层网络（也称为随机特征网络或极端学习机）被广泛用作数值试验空间，但对标准光滑sigmoid激活函数在一般维度下的最优Sobolev逼近能力至今未被解决。既有理论结果多局限于特定激活函数或特定维度，缺乏对一般光滑sigmoid激活的统一最优速率刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立确定性字典和随机字典的最优逼近速率理论。

Method: 本文的核心技术路径包括：（1）发展了一种sigmoid脊表示（sigmoidal ridge representation），将光滑sigmoid函数族统一表达为脊函数形式；（2）在方向-偏移空间中结合确定性或概率性求积方法构造字典；（3）对输出系数保持多项式控制以确保数值稳定性；（4）利用Kolmogorov宽度框架证明最优性。对于随机情形，使用独立参数采样及概率界分析，证明以高概率达到相同逼近阶。

Result: 建立了对光滑sigmoid激活类（包括tanh、logistic sigmoid、erf等）的最优Sobolev逼近速率M^{-(k-m)/d}，该速率在Kolmogorov宽度意义上对Sobolev球是最优的。随机字典在独立采样下以高概率达到相同逼近指数，仅需对数因子超采样。数值实验在广泛维度、目标正则性和Sobolev误差范数下均恢复出预测的代数速率。

Conclusion: 本文解决了光滑sigmoid激活浅层网络最优Sobolev逼近能力这一长期未决问题，给出了确定性字典和随机字典的统一最优速率理论，并附以数值验证。该工作为随机特征方法的理论分析提供了坚实支撑，对数值逼近和机器学习理论具有重要价值。

Abstract: Shallow networks with prescribed or randomly sampled hidden parameters are widely used as numerical trial spaces, yet their optimal Sobolev approximation power with standard smooth sigmoidal activations in general dimension remains unresolved. We establish the corresponding optimal rates for a class of smooth sigmoidal activations with Schwartz-class derivative decay, including $\tanh$, the logistic sigmoid, and the error function $erf$. We first construct deterministic direction--offset dictionaries with $M$ features such that every $u\in H^k(Ω)$ can be approximated with error of order $M^{-(k-m)/d}$ in $H^m(Ω)$ for all $0\le m\le k$. This rate is optimal in the sense of Kolmogorov widths for Sobolev balls. We further prove that dictionaries obtained by independent parameter sampling from any prescribed density bounded away from zero attain the same approximation exponent with high probability, up to logarithmic oversampling. The analysis develops a sigmoidal ridge representation and combines it with deterministic or probabilistic quadrature in direction--offset space while retaining polynomial control of the output coefficients. Numerical experiments across a broad range of dimensions, target regularities, and Sobolev error norms recover the predicted algebraic rates for both deterministic and random feature dictionaries.

</details>


### [112] [Sharp Dimension Bounds for Spline Spaces over T-meshes with Highest Order of Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19839)
*Bingru Huang,Falai Chen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了T网格上双次多项式样条空间的维数问题。在T连通分支不含可消失T边的前提下，通过引入解耦技术处理完全不可对角化分量（CNDC），将全局共形条件转化为沿内部大边的局部线性方程，提出了新的维数公式并得到了尖锐的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式样条空间维数取决于T网格的拓扑结构和几何配置，现有方法难以处理紧耦合的多顶点约束问题，需要新的技术来获得维数的精确估计。

Method: （1）引入CNDC（完全不可对角化分量）中的解耦技术，将全局共形条件转化为沿每条内部大边的局部线性方程；（2）基于此解耦技术提出新的维数公式；（3）从新公式推导出维数的尖锐上下界。

Result: 获得了维数的尖锐上下界，即相同拓扑但不同几何实现方式的T网格可分别达到维数的下界和上界；证明了新公式与Mourrain同调维数公式一致，且获得了更优的下界。

Conclusion: 通过解耦技术有效解决了T网格样条空间中多顶点约束紧耦合带来的维数分析困难，得到了理论保证的尖锐维数界，并验证了新方法与现有同调方法的一致性。

Abstract: The dimension of a polynomial spline space of bi-degree $(d_1,d_2)$ over a T-mesh $\mathscr{T}$ with the highest order of smoothness $(d_1-1,d_2-1)$ depends on both mesh topology and geometric configurations. Under the assumption that the T-connected components of the T-mesh $\mathscr{T}$ contain no vanishable T $l$-edges, we develop explicit upper and lower bounds of the dimension of the polynomial spline space. By introducing a decoupling technique within the completely non-diagonalizable component (CNDC) of the T-mesh $\mathscr{T}$, we separate tightly coupled multi-vertex constraints and transform global conformality conditions into localized linear equations along each interior large edge. Based on the decoupling technique, a new dimension formula of the polynomial spline space is then presented, and from which sharp upper and lower bounds of the dimension are obtained. The bounds are sharp in the sense that different geometric realizations of T-meshes with the same topology can attain the lower and upper bounds for the dimension of the polynomial spline space. We further prove that the new formula is consistent with Mourrain's homological dimension formula, and a sharper lower bound is obtained by our method.

</details>


### [113] [A Comprehensive p-VEM Framework for Advanced Variable Stiffness Plates with Arbitrary Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20034)
*Paola Pia Foligno,Daniele Boffi,Fabio Credali,Riccardo Vescovini*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个高阶（p版本）虚拟单元法（VEM）框架，用于变刚度板的结构分析。该框架采用熟悉的有限元法（FEM）记法，使实现易于被工程社区接受，并集成了标准稳定化和自稳定化策略，以及考虑变系数的多项式投影（VC-VEM方法）。该统一框架应用于线性静力、自由振动和屈曲分析，并通过解析解和数值基准进行验证，尤其针对含孔洞板和具有高梯度解的问题展示了其灵活性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟单元法（VEM）虽然能够处理任意多边形网格（包括曲边），非常适合复杂构型的结构分析，但其数学表述复杂，阻碍了其在工程社区的推广应用。此外，VEM在变系数问题中需要提高鲁棒性。因此，本文旨在提供一个使用熟悉的FEM记法的可访问实现，并集成新的高级能力以促进VEM在工程实际中的应用。

Method: 本文提出了一个统一的p版本VEM框架，具体包括：（1）采用标准FEM记法简化数学表述，便于工程实现；（2）同时集成标准稳定化策略和先进的自稳定化策略；（3）针对变系数问题，引入考虑系数的多项式投影，即变系数VEM（VC-VEM）方法；（4）将框架统一应用于线性静力、自由振动和屈曲三类分析。

Result: 通过解析解和数值基准验证，该框架在含孔洞板和高梯度解问题上表现良好，证明了所提出的综合方法为先进结构设计提供了灵活且易于实现（ready-to-implement）的工具。

Conclusion: 本文提出的VC-VEM综合框架成功弥合了VEM复杂数学表述与工程实际应用之间的差距，为变刚度板的结构分析提供了一种兼具灵活性、鲁棒性和可实施性的统一工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive, high-order (p-version) Virtual Element Method (VEM) framework for the structural analysis of innovative variable stiffness plates. VEM is particularly suited for complex configurations due to its ability to handle arbitrary polygonal meshes, including curved edges. However, its mathematical formulation may hinder its spread in the engineering community. This work illustrates a formulation with an accessible implementation using well-known FEM notation and integrating at the same time a set of new advanced capabilities. Specifically, both standard stabilized and advanced self-stabilized strategies are adopted. To further improve the robustness of VEM in the presence of variable coefficients, polynomial projections taking into account the coefficients are employed. This approach is referred to as Variable Coefficients-VEM approach (VC-VEM). This unified framework is applied to linear static, free-vibration, and buckling analyses, and validated against analytical solutions and numerical benchmarks. In particular, plates with cutouts and problems featuring high-gradient solutions are investigated, demonstrating that the proposed comprehensive approach provides a flexible and ready-to-implement tool for advanced structural design.

</details>


### [114] [Nearly tight framelet systems from nested Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund measures on compact Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20058)
*Hao-Ning Wu,Xiaosheng Zhuang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在紧致黎曼流形上提出嵌套Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund (MZ)测度作为多项式精确求积规则的定量替代方案，用于离散化紧框架小波系统。通过二进准均匀嵌套点集和局部划分权，建立了扩散多项式空间的确定性MZ不等式序列，并证明MZ容差直接控制框架界的偏离度、近紧性的改善速率以及滤波组变换的条件数。数值实验在球面和平面环面上验证了多尺度分解与重构行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流形上的紧框架小波系统具有精确能量保持和单遍重构的优势，但其标准半离散化依赖于多项式精确求积规则，这些规则难以与散乱且渐进细化的数据兼容。需要一种能够适应非均匀、嵌套数据结构的定量替代方案。

Method: 利用二进准均匀嵌套点集和局部划分权重，构造嵌套MZ测度序列。推导固定容差版本（多项式带宽随层级二进增长）和固定带宽细化版本（新增嵌套节点后MZ容差改善）两种互补形式的确定性MZ不等式。将MZ测度用于离散化连续紧框架小波，建立滤波组分析和综合过程，分析单遍与正则重构，并设计快速滤波组变换算法。

Result: MZ容差直接转移到框架上下界的偏差中，求积精确性被放宽为可控的紧性损失，且近紧性沿嵌套层级改善。MZ容差同样控制框架算子方程的条件数。在球面和平面环面上的数值实验展示了多尺度分解和重构效果。

Conclusion: 嵌套MZ测度为流形上紧框架小波的离散化提供了一种定量、确定性的框架，在保留能量保持和单遍重构优点的同时，解决了散乱渐进细化数据的求积困难。

Abstract: Tight framelet systems on manifolds provide exact energy preservation and one-pass reconstruction, but their standard semi-discretization relies on polynomial-exact quadrature rules, which are difficult to reconcile with scattered and progressively refined data. We develop nested Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund (MZ) measures on compact Riemannian manifolds as a quantitative alternative. Using dyadic quasi-uniform nested point sets and local partition weights, we establish a sequence of deterministic MZ inequalities for diffusion polynomial spaces. The result has two complementary forms: a fixed-tolerance version, in which the polynomial bandwidth grows dyadically with the level, and a fixed-bandwidth refinement version, in which the MZ tolerance improves as more nested nodes are added. When these measures are used to discretize continuous tight framelets, the MZ tolerance transfers directly to the deviation of the frame bounds from one. Thus quadrature exactness is relaxed with a controlled loss of tightness, and near-tightness improves along the same nested hierarchy. For the fully discrete setting, we develop filter-bank analysis and synthesis procedures, characterize one-pass and canonical reconstruction, and show that the MZ tolerance also controls the conditioning of the frame-operator equation. Fast implementation of filter-bank transforms is also presented. Numerical experiments on the sphere and the flat torus illustrate the resulting multiscale decompositions and reconstruction behavior.

</details>


### [115] [Domain derivative and shape reconstruction for an inverse backscattering problem for the wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20078)
*Roland Griesmaier,Marvin Knöller,Eliane Kummer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究三维无界空间中时域声波逆背散射问题，利用少量时变入射平面波对应的动态背散射远场数据重建可穿透散射体形状，建立了远场模式对形状的Fréchet可微性，并通过Laplace变换刻画时间域导数，进而结合卷积求积和边界元方法实现正则化Gauß-Newton算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆散射问题在雷达成像、无损检测、医学成像等领域有广泛应用。时域背散射问题因其测量配置简单（收发同置）而具有实际工程意义，需要发展高效的数值重建算法来从有限测量数据中恢复散射体形状。

Method: 本文首先建立时域远场模式关于散射体形状的Fréchet可微性，通过Laplace变换给出时间域形状导数的刻画。基于这一解析结果，利用卷积求积（CQ）方法处理时间离散，边界元方法（BEM）处理空间离散，实现正则化Gauß-Newton迭代算法用于反演散射体形状。

Result: 数值实验展示了所提算法的有效性及局限性，验证了基于Laplace变换的导数刻画在Gauß-Newton迭代中的高效性和可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作为时域逆背散射问题提供了一种理论上严格、数值上高效的形状重建算法框架，证明了利用少量时变入射波数据即可实现散射体重建，同时指出了算法在实际应用中的适用范围与限制。

Abstract: We consider an inverse backscattering problem for time-dependent acoustic waves with a compactly supported penetrable scattering obstacle in unbounded three-dimensional free space. Assuming that dynamic backscattering far field data of scattered waves corresponding to a few time-dependent incident plane waves are available, the goal is to reconstruct the shape of the scattering obstacle. We establish Fréchet differentiability of the time-dependent far field pattern with respect to the shape of the scattering obstacle and give a characterization of the associated temporal domain derivative in terms of its Laplace transform. This characterization is then utilized in an efficient implementation of a regularized Gauß-Newton method for the inverse backscattering problem using convolution quadrature and a boundary element method. Numerical examples demonstrate potentials and limitations of the algorithm.

</details>


### [116] [Superconvergence and aliasing saturation in Sloan iteration for spherical integral equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20098)
*Hao-Ning Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Sloan迭代在球面区域积分方程经求积离散后收敛阶的提升效果。通过球谐函数表达Sloan误差恒等式，揭示了求积离散中的尾项-混叠效应对四种方法的影响，给出了正权多项式精确求积下的锐利双向最坏情况界，并给出恢复-饱和阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sloan迭代能提高Galerkin和退化核近似求解第二类积分方程的收敛阶，但经求积离散后，这种提升有多少得以保留尚不清楚。理解求积离散对Sloan迭代效果的影响，是确保实际计算中收敛阶不损失的关键。

Method: 通过球谐函数将Sloan误差恒等式显式化，从频谱角度分析求积离散导致的混叠效应；对正权多项式精确求积和精确代数阶乘子，推导分离谱尾和混叠贡献的锐利双向最坏情况界；利用Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund稳定性与Gram校正最小二乘扩展至非多项式精确情形。

Result: 在无求积时Sloan迭代充分利用积分算子的光滑性；求积通过将未解析信息混叠到低频模式而破坏这种增益，进一步迭代不再提高渐近阶。推导了深度依赖的恢复-饱和阈值：充分过积分可恢复无求积速率，低于阈值时混叠决定锐利阶。数值实验验证了两个区域。

Conclusion: 给出了球面区域第二类积分方程四种方法（离散Galerkin、乘积积分Nyström及其Sloan迭代形式）统一的尾项-混叠描述，明确了求积离散下Sloan迭代增益损失的机制与恢复条件。

Abstract: Sloan iteration raises the convergence order of Galerkin and degenerate-kernel approximations to second-kind integral equations. After quadrature discretization, these become a discrete Galerkin method and a product-integration Nyström method, respectively. How much of this improvement survives quadrature discretization? For zonal integral equations on the sphere, we give a sharp answer by expressing the Sloan error identity in terms of spherical harmonics. In the absence of quadrature, Sloan iteration fully exploits the smoothing of the integral operator. Quadrature can destroy this gain by aliasing unresolved information into low-frequency modes, where further iteration no longer improves the asymptotic rate. This yields a unified tail--aliasing description of these four methods. For positive-weight polynomially exact quadrature and multipliers of exact algebraic order, we derive sharp two-sided worst-case bounds that separate the spectral-tail and aliasing contributions. Their balance yields a depth-dependent recovery--saturation threshold: sufficient overintegration recovers the quadrature-free rate, while below the threshold aliasing determines the sharp order. We also extend the analysis beyond polynomial exactness using Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund stability and Gram-corrected least squares. Numerical experiments illustrate both regimes.

</details>


### [117] [Implicit BDF2 dual time-stepping positivity-preserving entropy-stable schemes for unsteady compressible viscous flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20103)
*Mohammed Sayyari,Nail K. Yamaleev*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向三维可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的时间隐式格式，将2023年Upperman和Yamaleev开发的显式、高阶、保正性、熵稳定谱配点格式扩展到BDF2时间离散格式，并采用双时间步进（DTS）技术求解每个物理时间步的非线性方程组。该格式在伪时间上保持熵稳定和正性保持，在物理时间上具有无条件稳定性，数值结果验证了其对含强激波和接触间断的超声速流动的效率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式格式在高Reynolds数变粘性流动中受到严格的CFL稳定性条件限制，导致时间步长极小、计算效率低下。因此需要开发一种时间隐式格式，在保持原始格式的高阶精度、正性保持和熵稳定等有利性质的同时，克服显式格式的效率瓶颈。

Method: 将显式高阶熵稳定谱配点格式扩展到隐式时间积分框架。时间导数项采用二阶隐式后向差分公式（BDF2）离散，每个物理时间步产生的非线性方程组通过双时间步进（DTS）技术求解，该技术将物理时间内的隐式问题转化为伪时间内的稳态问题进行迭代求解。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的保正性BDF2 DTS格式相较于其显式对应格式，在含强激波和接触间断的超声速流动仿真中展现出更高的效率和精度。

Conclusion: BDF2 DTS格式在伪时间上保持熵稳定和正性保持性质，在物理时间上提供无条件稳定性，为高Reynolds数变粘性可压缩流动的高效高精度数值模拟提供了一种可靠的隐式时间积分方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a rigorous extension of the explicit, high-order, positivity-preserving, and entropy-stable spectral collocation schemes developed in Upperman 2023 and Yamaleev 2023 for the 3D compressible Navier-Stokes equations to a time-implicit formulation. The time derivative terms are discretized by using the second-order implicit backward difference formula (BDF2) that is well suited for solving time-variable viscous flows at high Reynolds numbers. The nonlinear system of discrete equations resulting from the BDF2 discretization at each physical timestep is solved using a dual time-stepping (DTS) technique. The BDF2 DTS scheme is entropy-stable and positivity-preserving in the pseudotime and provides unconditional stability properties in the physical time. Numerical results demonstrate the efficiency and accuracy of the positivity-preserving BDF2 DTS scheme as compared with its explicit counterpart are presented for supersonic flows with strong shock waves and contact discontinuities.

</details>


### [118] [Numerical Study of a Surface Growth Model with Singular Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20159)
*Dirk Blömker,David Buchberger,Johannes Rimmele*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由空间粗糙加性噪声驱动的外延薄膜生长随机模型，在噪声奇异且通过傅里叶空间截断进行正则化的情形下，分析了非线性项在极限中消失的现象，并推导了谱Galerkin离散与指数Euler时间积分方案的强误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外延薄膜生长过程中噪声的奇异性和空间粗糙性给数值模拟和理论分析带来挑战，需要理解空间截断、时间步长与非线性电流衰减之间的相互作用，以准确刻画从丘陵形成到噪声主导的动力学期望。

Method: 采用谱Galerkin投影对非线性项进行离散化，使用指数Euler格式进行时间积分；在L^p(Ω;C([0,T];H^1))空间中推导强误差估计，显式展示空间截断、时间步长与非线性电流衰减的交互作用；同时量化截断随机卷积的增长及非线性的消失速率，并通过数值实验验证。

Result: 获得了比空间-时间白噪声更粗糙噪声情形下的强误差估计，明确了各参数对收敛性的影响；数值实验展示了随着粗糙度参数增大，系统从持久丘陵形成向噪声主导动力学的转变。

Conclusion: 该研究为奇异粗糙噪声驱动的薄膜生长方程的数值逼近提供了严格的误差分析框架，并揭示了噪声粗糙度如何影响系统的非线性动力学行为。

Abstract: We study a stochastic model for epitaxial thin-film growth driven by spatially rough additive noise in a regime where the noise is singular and regularization via truncation in Fourier space is used to give a meaning to the solution, leading to a vanishing nonlinearity in the limit. In order to study this phenomenon numerically, the nonlinearity is discretized by a spectral Galerkin projection, while time integration is performed with an exponential Euler scheme. For roughness stronger than space-time white noise, we derive strong error estimates in $L^p(Ω;C([0,T];\mathcal H^1))$ that display explicitly the interaction between the spatial cut-off, the time step, and the decay of the nonlinear current.
  We also quantify the growth of the truncated stochastic convolution and the corresponding vanishing rate of the nonlinearity. Numerical experiments illustrate the transition from persistent hill formation to noise-dominated dynamics as the roughness parameter increases.

</details>
